So... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself doing things without thinking first? Like, do you act on impulse, interrupt people, or make quick decisions you later regret?\n\nPatient: I guess so. Uh... Is that restless feeling—that motor running inside you—still just as strong now as it was back then, or has it gotten better over the years?\n\nPatient: Uh... I don't really know. Ain't much for comparing, I guess. \n\nIt's... still there. Still always feel like I gotta be moving or doing something. So... guess it ain't got much better. Just... about the same.\n\nDoctor: Has this trouble with sitting still, focusing, and jumping in—has it caused you real problems in your life? I just end up letting everybody down, I guess. So... yeah. I just keep letting everybody down. So... yeah.
